Key audit result: V2 should not ship all 4 layers as proposed. The strongest issue is that this is symptom control with unknown generator source, plus Layer 2 appears to confuse Git ignore globs with regex quantifiers.

Sources checked: Git `.gitignore` pattern rules and `git clean` docs at git-scm.com, Lefthook jobs/config docs at lefthook.dev, Claude Code hooks schema at docs.claude.com/code.claude.com.

{
  "verdict": "PAUSE-FOR-ROOT-CAUSE",
  "confidence": 0.89,
  "v2_overclaims_caught": [
    {
      "claim": ".gitignore `/[a-z0-9_]{8}` implements root-only 8-char lowercase/digit/underscore ignore.",
      "actual": "Git ignore patterns are globs, not regex. `{8}` is literal-style text, not a quantifier. Git docs say `*`, `?`, and bracket ranges are supported; a leading slash anchors relative to that `.gitignore` level. Probe showed the expanded 8-bracket glob matches, while the quantifier form did not drive the match. Source: https://git-scm.com/docs/gitignore",
      "fix": "Use explicit glob lengths such as `/[a-z0-9_]`, `/[a-z0-9_][a-z0-9_]`, ... through 8, then verify with `git check-ignore -v` against representative root files and nested non-target paths."
    },
    {
      "claim": "Layer 3 lefthook pre-commit gate is an active protection layer.",
      "actual": "`lefthook.yml` is untracked and contains only the stock commented example. `core.hooksPath` is `.git/hooks`, `.git/hooks/pre-commit` is absent, and global `lefthook.exe` presence alone does not activate a hook. Lefthook jobs schema should be tied to official docs. Source: https://lefthook.dev/configuration/jobs",
      "fix": "Do not count Layer 3 unless a real `pre-commit.jobs[].run` config is committed and `lefthook install` or equivalent hook-path activation is verified."
    },
    {
      "claim": "Layer 4 `scripts/leak_audit.py` PostToolUse + SessionStart wire is ready as a runtime defense.",
      "actual": "`scripts/leak_audit.py` is missing locally. Claude Code hooks do support `PostToolUse` and `SessionStart` JSON-on-stdin, but V2 has not anchored output/input to the repo's audit-action-loop contract, and no local implementation exists. Source: https://docs.claude.com/en/docs/claude-code/hooks and https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks",
      "fix": "Either defer Layer 4 or first write a small hook with explicit JSONL schema, cite the sister contract, and probe it with captured hook input."
    },
    {
      "claim": "The 4-layer defense fixes the leak.",
      "actual": "It fixes or hides symptoms. Local tracked-source search did not find an obvious `blat` generator, but that does not prove the source is absent because shell history, external processes, hooks, and generated/untracked scripts remain possible.",
      "fix": "Treat source as `[UNKNOWN]`; run root-cause probes before durable ignore/hook layers."
    },
    {
      "claim": "1500 target files is the complete cleanup scope.",
      "actual": "Direct root probes found 1500 total 4-byte files, but 1503 root files matched length=4 plus `[a-z0-9_]{1,8}` name shape, and `git ls-files --others --exclude-standard` showed 1509 root names matching the proposed garbage/sh-typo classes. There are also `=1000`, `=10000`, `=500`, `nul`, `t`, and `tm` artifacts.",
      "fix": "Define the deletion predicate precisely and dry-run exact filenames before deletion."
    }
  ],
  "fm_class_risks_triggered": [
    "FM-09",
    "FM-20",
    "FM-15"
  ],
  "cr12_disposition": "PARTIAL-OVERLAP",
  "scope_down_recommendation": "PAUSE-FOR-ROOT-CAUSE",
  "sota_alternative_workflow": "First run root-cause discovery: inspect recent shell history/transcripts/hooks/processes around the artifact timestamps, then use Git-native dry-run discovery with `git ls-files --others --exclude-standard` and targeted `git check-ignore -v` probes. For cleanup, prefer a reversible review step with `git clean -n` or an explicit PowerShell dry-run list, then delete only the verified root garbage predicate. Add `.gitignore` root-anchored expanded globs only after proving they match target files and not legitimate repo-root files. Defer Lefthook and Claude hook audit until there is evidence the generator can recur.",
  "operator_recommendation": "ROOT-CAUSE-FIRST"
